# Idiomatic Rust audit report

Units audited: 342/342 (286068 of 286068 lines). Findings: 1105.
Verdicts: {'unverified': 951, 'systemic': 63, 'confirmed': 61, 'rejected': 11, 'needs-design': 19}. Haiku rejection rate among checked findings: 11/154 = 7%.

## Method

- Haiku 5.5 agents (effort medium) audited each unit against a condensed checklist (`CHECKLIST.md`). Units were capped at about 1,100 lines, which kept each agent under the 100K-token pricing tier.
- `verify.py` checks mechanically that each finding's evidence exists at the cited line. This catches misread or invented citations.
- Opus read the code for:
  - every high-severity finding;
  - a random sample of 20 medium/low findings (seed 7).
- The sample had 0 wrong findings and one overstated `public_api` flag. A second sample of 10 unreviewed high-severity allocation findings (seed 11) also looked correct; 2 of them were read in full against the code. Unverified medium/low findings and unreviewed high allocation findings are therefore treated as likely valid.
- Verdict meanings:
  - `systemic:<name>`: the finding belongs to one workspace-wide fix (see Systemic issues).
  - `needs-design`: the fix changes behavior or structure, or needs a benchmark first.

## Systemic issues

### S1 public-panic-contracts

Many public constructors and setters `assert!` on values app authors pass (Size::new, wheel/viewport/draggable extents, force-press pressures, tension, time dilation, drag boundaries, Canvas restore/finish, reactive mutation checks, memo cycles, CustomMultiChildLayout lookups). AGENTS.md requires a documented `# Panics` contract plus a `maybe_*`/`try_*` pair, or a structured error. Fix per type: add `# Panics` docs, add `try_new`/`maybe_*` returning a small typed error, keep the panicking form as a thin wrapper. Public API additions only (non-breaking). Findings tagged `systemic:public-panic-contracts`.

### S2 listener-snapshot

Listener lists are cloned into a fresh `Vec` on every notification so callbacks can re-enter (animation controller, scroll notifications, draggable sheet extents, back-gesture registry). Fix: store listeners as `Rc<[(u64, Listener)]>` rebuilt copy-on-write on subscribe/unsubscribe, so dispatch clones one `Rc`. Best as a small shared helper (e.g. `incular_core::listeners::ListenerList<F>`) used by every site. Behavior is identical (snapshot semantics preserved).

### S3 restoration-clone

Scroll state clones its `ScrollRestoration`/`RestorationKey` (owns a `String`) on every moved jump, ballistic tick and physics update to persist the offset. Fix: hold the restoration binding as `Rc<ScrollRestoration>` (or make the key `Rc<str>`) so persistence is a refcount bump, or persist through a borrow. Touches incular-scroll controller/activity/physics/coordinator.

### S4 route-outlet-frame

`route_outlet.rs` rebuilds route snapshots every frame: `Navigator::routes()` collects a `Vec<Route>` (called ~3x per frame), plus visibility Vec, live HashSet, order Vec, exiting clone, barrier clone and tag `Key::String` clones. Fix: add a borrowing accessor on Navigator (`with_routes(|&[Route]|)` / route ids), keep scratch collections on `RouteAnimations`, and compare revisions before rebuilding. Needs a frame benchmark before/after (navigation_showcase).

### S5 renderer-frame-scratch

The renderer and compositor allocate fresh containers each frame: lowering stacks, batch lists and instance Vecs, backdrop planning HashMaps, glyph page maps, composition bound stacks, leader publish maps, resource use lists. Fix: per-renderer scratch structs cleared and reused each frame. Restructuring, so per the audit rules it needs a benchmark (`benchmarks/`, performance_gallery) and is a design follow-up, not a mechanical edit.

### S6 layout-scratch

Several layout algorithms allocate per-layout scratch (wrap runs, table row heights, two-dimensional viewport child maps, animated/tree sliver position lists). Layout runs only for dirty subtrees, so cost is modest; fix with scratch fields on the render object or SmallVec. Benchmark before changing.

### S7 refcell-across-callback

RefCell borrows are held while calling app-supplied code (tree sliver row builder, back dispatcher, router provider subscription, draggable sheet physics). A callback that touches the same state panics with `BorrowMutError`. Fix: copy what is needed out of the borrow, drop it, then call. These are latent bugs, so fix them first.

### S8 id-generator

~20 copies of `AtomicU64::try_update` ID generators across crates (core context/key/reactivity, runtime, widgets, image, rendering, scroll, devtools). Fix: one `incular_core::id::next_id(&AtomicU64)` helper or a `IdGenerator` type; mechanical.

Identical — *same clamp-panic class as A065-01*
- `A248-01` [crates/incular-widgets/src/probe.rs:7](crates/incular-widgets/src/probe.rs#L7) repo:wasm32-clock high **public** — `pub fn span(slot: usize) -> Span { ⏎     Span(slot, Instant::now())` → Delete probe.rs (marked TEMPORARY, not for commit), its `pub mod probe;` line in src/lib.rs, the call sites tree/reconciliation.rs (span(1) near line 991, span(4) near line 2306) and scrolling/layout.rs (span(0) near line 2968), and benches/scroll_probe.rs. If the timer must stay, use incular_core:: — *REAL BUG - committed TEMPORARY probe.rs calls Instant::now in layout/reconcile hot paths (wasm panic + per-frame overhead); delete probe.rs, its mod line, 5 call sites and benches/scroll_probe.rs*
- `A270-04` [crates/incular-widgets/src/selection.rs:256](crates/incular-widgets/src/selection.rs#L256) §1 high **public** — `pub fn add_geometry_listener(&self, listener: impl Fn(SelectionGeometry) + 'static) -> u64 { ⏎ // stores Rc<dyn Fn(SelectionGeometry)>; called with geometry.clo` → The callback takes SelectionGeometry by value, so every geometry change clones the Vec<Rect> once per geometry listener (line 365 `listener(geometry.clone());`) even though listeners only read it. Change to `impl Fn(&SelectionGeometry) + 'static`, store `Rc<dyn Fn(&SelectionGeometry)>` (line 157), a — *public listener signature &SelectionGeometry*

## Design follow-ups


### incular-gestures

- `A063-01` [crates/incular-gestures/src/keyboard.rs:1183](crates/incular-gestures/src/keyboard.rs#L1183) §2 high  — `let logical = LogicalShortcutKey::new(event.key.clone(), event.modifiers);` → Every key event clones event.key, which heap-allocates for Key::Character(String). Key the logical map so lookups borrow the event key: change ShortcutScope.logical to HashMap<Modifiers, HashMap<KeyboardKey, Vec<ShortcutRegistration<C>>>> and look up with scope.logical.get(&event.modifiers).and_then — *per-keystroke String clone; changing map key type is wider than it looks*
- `A063-02` [crates/incular-gestures/src/keyboard.rs:1221](crates/incular-gestures/src/keyboard.rs#L1221) §2 high  — `ShortcutBinding::Intent(intent) => { ⏎ ShortcutMatch::Intent(ErasedIntent::from_intent(intent))` → find_match runs per key event for each ancestor scope (via ErasedShortcutScope::find from focus_keyboard.rs). Each call here allocates an Rc and clones the command (a String for Command). Build the erased form once at registration: store ShortcutBinding::Intent(Intent<C>, ErasedIntent) in bind_on an — *per key event Rc; not per frame*

### incular-rendering

- `A118-01` [crates/incular-rendering/src/compositor.rs:1735](crates/incular-rendering/src/compositor.rs#L1735) §2 high **public** — `let Some(kind) = self.layers.get(id.0).map(|layer| layer.kind.clone()) else { ⏎  return; ⏎  };` → Make the boxed payloads reference-counted so the per-layer clone is a refcount bump: in compositor.rs change `LayerKind::ColorFilter { filter: Box<ColorFilter> }` to `filter: Arc<ColorFilter>` and `LayerKind::ShaderMask { shader: Box<Brush>, .. }` to `shader: Arc<Brush>` (update the constructors/upd — *LayerKind clone per node per frame to dodge &mut self borrow; restructure + benchmark*
- `A119-03` [crates/incular-rendering/src/compositor.rs:2202](crates/incular-rendering/src/compositor.rs#L2202) §2 high  — `let Some(kind) = self.layers.get(id.0).map(|layer| layer.kind.clone()) else { ⏎     return; ⏎ }; ⏎ match kind {` → Avoid cloning the whole LayerKind per node per frame. Copy only the small fields each arm needs and borrow nothing across the `&mut self` recursive call, e.g. match on a copied discriminant plus the copied fields: `let Some(layer) = self.layers.get(id.0) else { return; }; match &layer.kind { LayerKi — *same pattern as A118-01*
- `A120-01` [crates/incular-rendering/src/display_list.rs:453](crates/incular-rendering/src/display_list.rs#L453) §2 high  — `entry.extend_from(&Self::lift_partitions_in(&commands[start + 1..end]));` → Write the recursion straight into the output instead of building an intermediate list and cloning it: change `fn lift_partitions_in(commands: &[PaintCommand]) -> Self` to `fn lift_partitions_into(commands: &[PaintCommand], out: &mut Self)`, push base commands into `out`, collect nested lifted entrie — *recursive partition lift allocates; restructure*
- `A120-03` [crates/incular-rendering/src/display_list.rs:513](crates/incular-rendering/src/display_list.rs#L513) §2 high **public** — `let mut transforms = vec![Transform::IDENTITY];` → Avoid a heap allocation on every call: use an inline small stack, e.g. `let mut transforms: smallvec::SmallVec<[Transform; 8]> = smallvec::smallvec![Transform::IDENTITY];` (smallvec is already a workspace dependency, root Cargo.toml line 84; add `smallvec = { workspace = true }` to crates/incular-re — *SmallVec transform stack; check call frequency*
- `A122-01` [crates/incular-rendering/src/effects.rs:874](crates/incular-rendering/src/effects.rs#L874) §2 high **public** — `pub fn stages(&self) -> Vec<Self> { ⏎     match self { ⏎         Self::Compose { outer, inner } => {` → Stop allocating and cloning per query. Visit stages by reference with a callback or a small borrowed iterator, e.g. `pub fn for_each_stage<'a>(&'a self, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&'a ImageFilter)) { match self { Self::Compose { outer, inner } => { inner.for_each_stage(visit); outer.for_each_stage(visit — *stages() Vec is public; add a borrowing visitor*

### incular-runtime

- `A149-01` [crates/incular-runtime/src/window_commands.rs:85](crates/incular-runtime/src/window_commands.rs#L85) §2 medium **public** — `pub(crate) fn all(&self) -> Vec<DisplaySnapshot> { ⏎     self.displays.values().cloned().collect()` → Store snapshots as shared handles so reads clone a pointer, not the snapshot's `name: Option<String>`: change the map to `BTreeMap<DisplayId, Arc<DisplaySnapshot>>` (and `primary` lookups stay as is), have `all()` return `Vec<Arc<DisplaySnapshot>>` and `get()`/`primary()` return `Option<Arc<DisplayS — *pub(crate), Arc snapshot is a design choice*

### incular-scroll

- `A153-02` [crates/incular-scroll/src/extent_index.rs:405](crates/incular-scroll/src/extent_index.rs#L405) §2 high **public** — `pub fn offsets_for_range(&self, range: std::ops::Range<usize>) -> Vec<f32> { ⏎         self.metrics ⏎             .index_to_offset` → Let the caller own the buffer: pub fn offsets_for_range_into(&self, range: std::ops::Range<usize>, out: &mut Vec<f32>) { out.clear(); out.reserve(range.len() + 1); self.state.borrow().offsets_for_range(range, out); }. Change the private MeasuredExtentState::offsets_for_range to take out: &mut Vec<f3 — *public Vec return; check callers before adding _into form*

### incular-text

- `A158-01` [crates/incular-text/src/engine.rs:1439](crates/incular-text/src/engine.rs#L1439) §2 high  — `let clipped_runs = clip_runs(runs, end, candidate.len());` → Stop rebuilding owned runs on every ellipsis iteration. clip_runs (engine.rs ~2016-2023) clones run.text and run.style for every run each loop pass, but shape_paragraph only reads run.style and run.range. Change shape_raw/shape_paragraph to take a borrowed run view (range plus &TextStyle) so clip_ru — *ellipsis search rebuilds runs per iteration*
- `A158-06` [crates/incular-text/src/engine.rs:967](crates/incular-text/src/engine.rs#L967) §11 high **public** — `let mut layout = self.shape_paragraph("M", &style, None, None, &[]);` → resolve_strut (called per laid-out text with a strut, incular-widgets tree/layout/behavior/text.rs:50) shapes the "M" probe paragraph and clones base on every call. Memoize the resolved TextStrut keyed by the strut key plus the base style fields it reads, and clear that memo with the other pools in  — *cache strut probe per style*
- `A161-01` [crates/incular-text/src/geometry.rs:218](crates/incular-text/src/geometry.rs#L218) §2 high **public** — `pub fn selection_spans(&self, start: usize, end: usize) -> Vec<(f32, f32)> { ⏎ if end < self.byte { ⏎ return Vec::new();` → Stop returning a fresh Vec per call. Either push into a caller-owned buffer: `pub fn selection_spans(&self, start: usize, end: usize, out: &mut Vec<(f32, f32)>)` (clear and reuse `out`), or return `impl Iterator<Item = (f32, f32)> + 'a` built on the shaped line's spans. The wrapper delegates to `Tex — *public Vec return in selection geometry*
- `A163-01` [crates/incular-text/src/style/text_style.rs:618](crates/incular-text/src/style/text_style.rs#L618) §11 high **public** — `locale: if t < 0.5 { ⏎                 self.locale.clone() ⏎             } else {` → Change the public fields `locale: Option<String>` (line 31) and `debug_label: Option<String>` (line 41) to `Option<Arc<str>>` so that the per-frame Lerp::lerp (lines 618-622 and 708-712), merge (403, 386 area), apply, scaled and with_* clones are refcount bumps instead of String allocations. Setters — *TextStyle String fields to Arc<str> is a public API change*
- `U106-01` [crates/incular-text/src/boundary.rs:178](crates/incular-text/src/boundary.rs#L178) §2 high **public** — `for boundary in word_boundaries(text) {` → Segment once per public call and pass the slice down. Change word_range_at(text, position) to word_range_in(text_len, boundaries: &[usize], position) and build the Vec once in word_leading/word_trailing before the loop: let boundaries = word_boundaries(text); then call word_range_in(text.len(), &bou — *segment once and pass boundaries; public signature change*

### incular-wgpu

- `A178-01` [crates/incular-wgpu/src/renderer/effects.rs:952](crates/incular-wgpu/src/renderer/effects.rs#L952) §11 high  — `let bind_group = self.device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor { ⏎  label: Some("incular transient effect bind group"), ⏎  layout: &self.blur_bind_gr` → Create the bind group once per (source view, params slot) and reuse it: keep a small cache keyed by the source texture revision (or the OffscreenTarget identity) in the renderer, and invalidate it on device_generation change. - `A182-01` [crates/incular-wgpu/src/renderer/rendering.rs:643](crates/incular-wgpu/src/renderer/rendering.rs#L643) §11 high  — `let bind_group = self.device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor { ⏎     label: Some("incular destination blend bind group"), ⏎     layout: &self.blend` → Create the destination-blend bind group once per (source_view, destination_view) pair and reuse it while the offscreen/effect targets are unchanged. Store it in a cache owned by the renderer (keyed by layer and source kind, invalidated when the target texture is recreated or the destination view cha — *same as A178-01*

### incular-widgets

- `A194-04` [crates/incular-widgets/src/advanced_slivers/animated.rs:639](crates/incular-widgets/src/advanced_slivers/animated.rs#L639) §11 high  — `(0..position) ⏎     .map(|index| self.list_entry_extent(index)) ⏎     .sum()` → list_offset_for is O(position) and is called once per materialized child in list_layout (line 673), making layout O(n*m) with a HashMap lookup per step. Precompute the offset in one pass: since `positions` is sorted ascending and deduped, keep a running cursor in list_layout: `let mut scanned = 0; l — *O(visible*n) offset sums; use prefix sums or the extent index*
- `A205-01` [crates/incular-widgets/src/custom_layout.rs:259](crates/incular-widgets/src/custom_layout.rs#L259) §2 high **public** — `pub fn has_child(&self, id: impl Into<LayoutChildId>) -> bool { ⏎         self.ids.contains_key(&id.into())` → Every lookup builds an owned LayoutChildId: From<&str> allocates an Rc<str> and From<usize> allocates a String via to_string(). layout_child (267) and position_child (277) do the same once per child per multi-child layout. Make the lookup key borrow: add `pub enum LayoutKey<'a> { Name(&'a str), Inde — *LayoutChildId lookup allocates; Borrow-based lookup changes public API*
- `A296-03` [crates/incular-widgets/src/tree/reconciliation.rs:911](crates/incular-widgets/src/tree/reconciliation.rs#L911) §19 high  — `if let Some(key) = self ⏎     .elements ⏎     .get(id.0) ⏎     .and_then(|e| e.widget.key().cloned())` → Avoid cloning every old key (String keys allocate). Index the desired middle keys by reference instead: build `HashMap<&Key, usize>` from `desired_middle` (keys are unique, validated by check_keys_borrowed), then scan `old_middle` once and fill a `Vec<Option<(ElementId, usize)>>` indexed by desired  — *reconciliation keyed diff; benchmark*
